Open questions the thread left (carried into this research):

- Which autofocus technology and supplier did Meta select for the spring-2027 VR Glasses' stereo passthrough cameras? Meta's specs confirm autofocus and depth sensing but name neither actuator nor supplier; no teardown, supplier disclosure or poLight design-win/mass-production announcement tied to a U.S. top-tier OEM has appeared.
- Which, if any, of poLight's three separate consumer programs is Meta: A (Q Tech-backed top-tier U.S. OEM qualification), B (Oct-2025 ~NOK 5m AR head-worn camera project), or C (2026 consumer AR/MR design-in whose product is expected in 2026)? Could the Q Tech-backed OEM be Meta but for a different generation/model?
- Is the Q Tech-backed top-tier U.S. OEM Meta, Apple, Google, Snap, Amazon or another company? poLight has never named Meta.
- Who is the customer behind the Oct-2025 NOK 5m AR camera project, did the modules shipped end-Q2 2026 pass validation, and is it a platform/ecosystem company (e.g. Android XR/Google)? Isaksen declined to explain the ecosystem arrangement.
- Who is the 2026 consumer AR/MR design-in customer whose product launches in 2026, and was that product delayed?
- Is Q Tech's 2024 'US M brand' XR vendor-code customer the same top-tier U.S. OEM that backed the 2025 Q Tech-poLight investment? No primary-source sentence found saying yes; can a Q Tech report, transcript or Q&A be found that links them?
- Is Q Technology a camera-module supplier to Meta's Reality Labs/Quest hardware (even historically), and is it the passthrough-camera module supplier for the 2027 VR Glasses?
- Does the DBS-described Meta + Q Tech + poLight H1 2027 mass-production project correspond specifically to the spring-2027 Meta VR Glasses or to a different Meta glasses product, and what is DBS's underlying source?
- When will the Q Tech-backed OEM's production ramp begin, what volumes does it need (Q Tech's initial line is not sized for eventual volume), and has product-level (camera/headset) qualification been completed as distinct from component-level qualification of Q Tech-made lenses?
- Will the next Q Tech/top-tier OEM order jump from NOK 0.7-1m qualification batches to several million NOK (assistant expects a first production PO of ~NOK 5-15m by late Q4 2026 / Q1 2027) with wording shifting from 'qualification' to 'production/ramp'? Has poLight upgraded the Q Tech case to design-in or mass production?
- What is the cumulative 2026 order flow from the Q Tech-backed OEM, and which (if any) TWedge POs belong to that OEM and would raise the attributable total above NOK 2.5m?
- Does Meta's granted patent US 12,663,619 (citing 2022 poLight documents) reflect in-house development, licensing of poLight technology, or a supply relationship? Who introduced the poLight references (applicant, examiner or search), did poLight's prior art cause the narrowing amendments, and would use of the underlying technology require a poLight license?
- Does Meta's production passthrough camera actually use the PZT/polymer/membrane construction claimed in US12663619 (constant FOV during focusing), or a VCM/SMA/MEMS architecture from Meta's other filings? The patent-to-production link is missing.
- Do the Meta patent specifications (5.0 mm TTL, ~20 cm minimum focus, <1 mm BFL) differ from poLight's public 6.8 mm TTL / 10 cm reference design because of a different design, a customized/in-house tunable element, or a further-developed poLight part?
- Is the 'via' electrical feed-through in US20260276939A1 a Meta-specific novelty compatible with a bare poLight TLens, or does it indicate a different (in-house or third-party) PZT-polymer lens supplier?
- Who supplied the piezoelectric tunable lens in the Meta 'miniature piezoelectric tunable lens AF camera module' described on the LinkedIn profile (poLight/TLens vs another piezo supplier)? Was that module selected for the spring-2027 glasses, or did Meta evaluate alternatives in parallel? What does the full profile say and who is the individual?
- Are the LinkedIn module's reliability tests and poLight's still-ongoing Q Tech qualification (Feb 2026) different levels/revisions or different projects?
- Does anything publicly exclude CML as the autofocus supplier for Meta's 2027 glasses? Are CML's 2021 'US technology giants' engagements and 2023 XR commercial projects the same customers, still ongoing, and about camera AF or display optics? Does CML's latest financial accounts PDF (not retrieved) contain relevant contract disclosures?
- Could CML supply a different function in the glasses (display-optics focus, OIS) even if not the piezo AF? Would CML's Zero Hold Power actually beat TLens on energy in continuous stereo-passthrough AF, and are there measurements of whether a current CML SMA actuator meets Meta's speed/power requirements?
- What is Meta's actual mechanical/electrical layout, needed to show whether CML's 5 mm sensor-to-driver spacing requirement conflicts with the module?
- Why did Meta let US20230280637A1 lapse after allowance (full USPTO file wrapper not read)?
- Was Optotune's EL-3.1-10.8 (launched Sept 2026, referenced in 2026 dev kits, datasheet updated 18 Sept 2026) available early enough to be designed into a spring-2027 device, and does Optotune have any undisclosed OEM program with Meta? Is there any product-specific comparison showing Optotune or a VCM competing for Meta's actual stereo-passthrough camera?
- Is there a second (alternative) autofocus supplier in the Meta program, the factor cited for keeping interim estimates below 80%?
- Will Meta publish production camera specifications (PZT membrane AF, ~1 ms response, constant FOV, single-digit mW, tens-of-volts drive) that would confirm the TLens fingerprint, or will a teardown identify the component?
- What are the actual battery capacity and camera power figures for the Meta VR Glasses, and how much battery life would TLens realistically add versus VCM (assistant offered to estimate but did not)?
- How many TLenses per Meta headset (assistant assumed two for stereo autofocus passthrough)?
- What are Meta's actual first-year VR Glasses volumes (assistant range 150k-900k, central 400k) and does Meta order components ahead of sell-through?
- If TLens is confirmed in Meta VR Glasses, how many future camera-equipped Meta glasses would reuse it (platform vs single SKU), and what volumes/valuation would adoption across several Meta glasses programs in a 2027 'year of AI/AR glasses' imply?
- Which Meta patent application(s) did the deep-research tool find at [133], and what did the deep-research answer (triggered at [130]) conclude about the biggest factors for/against poLight? (Answer not contained in the extracted chunks.)
- Could any supplier linkage, teardown or Aria Gen 2 technical documentation provide direct evidence of a tunable lens in Meta devices?
- Exact dates not stated in the thread: Meta's developer-spec publication date; exact date of the equity-investment completion (June 2025); exact date of poLight management's 'many years' commentary.

## 9. Coverage, gaps and method

Signals by source type: company 338, press 184, patent_office 56, other 42, exchange 33, analyst 27, forum 9.

Method: nine research agents compiled dated timelines per producer and supplier from company newsrooms, Oslo Børs / HKEX / SEC filings, Google Patents, developer documentation and reputable press, with forum and unnamed-source items marked as rumour; a digest of the earlier ChatGPT thread supplied the starting hypotheses; the rows were converted to a common schema, deduplicated per lane, cross-matched against launch windows, criticised for completeness, and the load-bearing dates were re-checked against their cited sources.

Completeness critic's list of remaining gaps:

```
analysts | 12 of 34 events (analysts-9-80..9-104: "Assistant probability table ~62%", "User creates scheduled task", "User uploads screenshot", "Assistant 2027-2029 revenue scenarios") are chat-trace artefacts, not market events; analysts-9-104 is dated 2027 and analysts-9-105 (Omdia forecast) is dated by forecast year, so both sort as future events | Move them to a separate "research-log" lane or drop; re-date forecasts to publication date (Omdia 2026-09-22); keep only sourced analyst notes (DBS, Omdia, Counterpoint, Everbright, Patsnap)

polight | Massive cross-digest duplication: the 2025-10-13 NOK 5m PO appears 6 times (polight-1-0, 4-48, 6-76, 7-28, 8-54, 9-36), each Program A/B PO 3-4 times; 179 events and 74 "key" flags are inflated and the same applies to metaip (US 12,663,619 grant = metaip-6-63/8-70/9-56) and windows (VR Glasses spring 2027 = w-meta-0-8/3-14/4-12/6-3/7-9/8-9/9-0; CMD = w-polight-4-6/7-4/8-6; DBS = w-analysts-4-13/7-8/9-1) | Run a dedupe pass on (lane, date, normalised title) keeping the best-sourced copy and merging ids; report true counts

polight | Finding 2 is arithmetically inconsistent: 18.8 and 8.4 months are outside the stated 9-18-month qualification band, yet the finding says all three POs are "inside" it | Rewrite: PO #1 precedes the band, PO #2 sits inside, PO #3 sits in the 3-9-month ramp zone; or justify a wider band from the Magic Leap 2 / Vuzix Shield precedents in the file

polight | Precondition missing from findings: AR2025 (polight-6-74) and Q2 2026 (polight-9-49) both say no new MEMS wafers were ordered, manufactured or delivered; a spring-2027 volume launch needs ST wafer orders roughly 6-9 months ahead, so "no wafers by 30 Jun 2026" is a stronger timing signal than any PO | Add a derived window "poLight wafer order to ST (Q3-Q4 2026)" and a checkpoint on the Q3 report (2026-10-29): search report text for "wafer"; query ST thin-film PZT lead times

meta | Finding 6 asserts Ray-Ban Meta Gen 3 "remains fixed focus" but no event supports it (meta-0-69 lists only 12MP/3K/9 h); only Gen 2 is documented fixed focus (meta-0-44) | Query Meta/Ray-Ban Gen 3 spec page and reviews (PCMag, The Verge, DCW) for "autofocus"/"fixed focus"; same for Meta Ray-Ban Display and Oakley Vanguard cameras, whose teardowns (meta-7-27, meta-7-22) give no AF detail

modules | Contradiction not addressed: the Phoenix supply-chain preview (modules-0-65) names Sunny Optical for camera modules, while the TLens story runs through Q Tech; Sunny H1-2026 also lists a "Compact High-Res AF" smart-glasses module (modules-7-53, truncated) with an unnamed actuator | Read Sunny Optical H1 2026 results deck for the AF module's actuator type; check whether modules-0-65 distinguishes passthrough from tracking cameras; query "舜宇 智能眼镜 自动对焦 模组"

qtech | Finding 4 conflates signals: "Volume Delivery Achieved" for TLens in H1 2026 is 9-12 months before spring 2027, well ahead of the 2-4-month module-MP norm, and fits a 2026 product (Program B) better than VR Glasses; the "US M brand vendor code" (2024) covers Q Tech's pancake lenses and modules generally, not TLens | Split the Q Tech signals by product (TLens vs AR Mono Engine vs pancake lens) using the H1-2026 deck pages; downgrade the vendor-code match

metaip | Tension unexamined: Lidu Huang's profile says Meta's piezo tunable-lens module passed AR/VR/MR reliability qualification before Apr 2025, yet Program A "qualification" POs only start Aug 2025; either Program A is not Meta, or the Aug-2025 POs are second-source (Q Tech line) qualification, which poLight-9-28 ("demanded dual-source") hints at | Add this as an explicit finding; search Meta job postings 2025-2026 for "tunable lens"/"piezo" camera roles and current employers of the other inventors (Xiong et al.)

samsung | samsung-1-56 (VRARA, "early reports" of 12MP with autofocus) contradicts findings 3 and 10 ("none has disclosed its focus type"); also Haean display glasses 2027 (w-samsung-1-18/1-19) has no supplier mapping while w-google-1-13 (same device family) does | Check FCC internal photos / Samsung spec page for SM-O200P "autofocus"; add w-samsung-1-18 to the lead-time windows with the same suppliers as w-google-1-13

rokid | Same gap: 41% share claimant (rokid-2-70) has no 2026-2027 roadmap window and no supplier events | Search "Rokid Glasses 2" / "Rokid 2027" rumours and Rokid's module supplier (Goertek? Q Tech?); add a window

xiaomi | Lane mixes two entities: xiaomi-6-38..41 are Nextlens (Swiss tunable-lens maker) events, not Xiaomi; Xiaomi AI Glasses (Jun 2025, IMX681) focus type is unstated | Move Nextlens events to a competitor lane; check Xiaomi AI Glasses spec page for "自动对焦"

cml | Finding 7 ("gone quiet") is partly contradicted by in-file events: WO2026087900A1 for glasses/VR/AR published 2026-04-30 (cml-5-69), a 2026 "AI Vision Product Manager" ad citing AI glasses (cml-5-74), and two 2026 share allotments suggesting a funding round | Soften to "no public XR customer signal since May 2025"; try to retrieve CML FY2025 accounts (filed 2026-03-10) for segment commentary (analysts-9-100 failed)

meta | w-meta-0-7 (Hypernova 2, H2 2026-2027) is a chosen lead-time window although meta-0-74 (Bosworth, 2026-09-24: "no new version" of Display hardware) undercuts it; and no explicit fixed-focus evidence exists for Quest 3 / Vision Pro / Galaxy XR passthrough to support finding 1's "only device with documented autofocus passthrough" | Mark w-meta-0-7 conflicting/superseded; add iFixit Quest 3 and Vision Pro passthrough-camera notes (fixed focus) as events

polight | Finding 3's "four POs" treats the 2025-11-24 NOK 0.9m PO from a "Leading Consumer OEM" (polight-4-58) as Program B, but poLight itself counts "≈NOK 4.0m over 3 orders" (polight-4-84/7-49); also the finding cites Q4 2026 deliveries whereas the ramp actually started with "deliveries from May 2026" (polight-2-128), which is what makes a fall-2026 launch fit the 3-6-month norm | Label the Nov-2025 PO linkage as inference; cite May 2026 as ramp start

windows | 20+ "windows" have start dates in the past or are superseded (w-metaip-0-18, w-apple-1-1/1-2/7-5/7-6, w-xreal-2-5/2-6, w-rokid-2-7/2-8, w-rayneo-2-9/10/11, w-cml-8-2, w-qtech-5-1, w-meta-0-12/0-14/0-16, w-polight-8-3/8-4); w-alibaba-3-3 (on sale in 7 days) and w-otheroem-3-8 (Q4 2026) are mapped to suppliers although sourcing is long closed | Tag past/superseded windows as "historical" and exclude them from lead-time matching; keep only windows whose supplier-decision point is still ahead
```

*Sources: poLight/Q Tech filings and decks, patent registers, Meta developer docs, press and broker notes as captured in the dataset (704 events, 108 windows after de-duplication). Phase bands apply generic 3-18-month industry norms, not disclosed schedules. poLight has never named Meta or said '2027'; the DBS 1H27 sentence is unverified; LinkedIn and forum items are circumstantial.*
